declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t706 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/multiasync.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t708 = alloca i32
  %t707 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t706, ptr %t708, ptr null)
  %t709 = icmp ne i32 %t707, 0
  br i1 %t709, label %main.async.fail.80, label %main.async.ok.80
main.async.fail.80:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.80:
  %t710 = load i32, ptr %t708
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t710
}
declare void @willrt_task_error_message(ptr, ptr, ptr)
declare i32 @willrt_task_error_status(ptr)
define internal { ptr, i64 } @will.task.error.message(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %t711 = alloca ptr
  %t712 = alloca i64
  call void @willrt_task_error_message(ptr %data, ptr %t711, ptr %t712)
  %t713 = load ptr, ptr %t711
  %t714 = load i64, ptr %t712
  %t715 = insertvalue { ptr, i64 } undef, ptr %t713, 0
  %t716 = insertvalue { ptr, i64 } %t715, i64 %t714, 1
  ret { ptr, i64 } %t716
}
define internal i32 @will.task.error.status(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %t717 = call i32 @willrt_task_error_status(ptr %data)
  ret i32 %t717
}
define internal ptr @will.task.error.clone(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %t718 = getelementptr %willrt.task_error, ptr null, i32 1
  %t719 = ptrtoint ptr %t718 to i64
  %t720 = call ptr @willrt_alloc(i64 %t719)
  call void @memcpy(ptr %t720, ptr %data, i64 %t719)
  %t721 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  %t722 = load ptr, ptr %t721
  %t723 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 0, i32 1
  %t724 = load i64, ptr %t723
  %t725 = icmp ne ptr %t722, null
  %t726 = icmp ugt i64 %t724, 0
  %t727 = and i1 %t725, %t726
  br i1 %t727, label %task.error.copy.81, label %task.error.empty.81
task.error.copy.81:
  %t728 = call ptr @willrt_alloc(i64 %t724)
  call void @memcpy(ptr %t728, ptr %t722, i64 %t724)
  %t729 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %t720, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  store ptr %t728, ptr %t729
  br label %task.error.msg.done.81
task.error.empty.81:
  %t730 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %t720, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  store ptr null, ptr %t730
  br label %task.error.msg.done.81
task.error.msg.done.81:
  ret ptr %t720
}
define internal void @will.task.error.drop(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %t731 = getelementptr inbounds %willrt.task_error, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 0, i32 0
  %t732 = load ptr, ptr %t731
  call void @willrt_free_str(ptr %t732)
  store ptr null, ptr %t731
  ret void
}
